    rule(true, "hide", "attribute", "UnitOfMeasure", "value", "CPM");

type RealTimeIDService : LineItem {
    string RevenueType = ["Fixed", "One-Time", "Variable"];

    string UnitOfMeasure = ["None", "Real Time Identity Service Responses", "Real Time Identity Service Requests", "Tokens", "CPM"];

    @(configurable = true, defaultValue = "Only returned RampIDs - $0.50 CPM")
    string Call_Type_V2 = ["Only returned RampIDs - $0.50 CPM", "All calls - $0.10 CPM", "None"];

    int qty = this.quantity;

    rule(true, "disable", "attribute", "Call_Type_V2");

    message(RevenueType == "Variable" && qty != 1, "When Revenue Type is Variable, quantity must be set to 1.", "error");

    rule(true, "hide", "attribute", "UnitOfMeasure", "value", "Tokens");

    rule(true, "hide", "attribute", "UnitOfMeasure", "value", "CPM");

    rule(true, "hide", "attribute", "Call_Type_V2", "value", "None");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure == "Real Time Identity Service Responses" -> Call_Type_V2 == "Only returned RampIDs - $0.50 CPM");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure == "Real Time Identity Service Requests" -> Call_Type_V2 == "All calls - $0.10 CPM");

    message(UnitOfMeasure == "None", "Please select a valid Unit of Measure.", "error");

    //CRM-43551

    rule(UnitOfMeasure == "Tokens", "hide", "attribute", "UnitOfMeasure");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure == "CPM", "hide", "attribute", "UnitOfMeasure");

    //CPM

    rule(UnitOfMeasure == "Tokens", "hide", "attribute", "RevenueType");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure == "CPM", "hide", "attribute", "RevenueType");

}

@(peelable = true)
type PseudonymousIdentityTranslationCloudEmbedded : LineItem {
    @(defaultValue = "0.00", peelable = true)
    decimal(2) Monthly_Minimum1;

    @(defaultValue = "0.00", peelable = true)
    decimal(2) Rev_Share1;

    @(relatedAttributes = "Rev_Share1, Monthly_Minimum1, Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency")
    string RevenueType = ["Fixed", "One-Time", "Variable"];

    @(defaultValue = "None", peelable = true)
    string Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency = ["Monthly", "Quarterly", "Annual", "None"];

    string Input_Records_V2 = ["None", "25M Input Records", "50M Input Records", "75M Input Records", "100M Input Records", "250M Input Records", "500M Input Records", "750M Input Records", "1B Input Records", "One Partner Unlimited"];

    @(relatedAttributes = "Rev_Share1, Monthly_Minimum1,Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency")
    string UnitOfMeasure = ["None", "Revenue Share", "Input Records", "Tokens", "CPM"];

    int qty = this.quantity;

    string parentName = parent(BundleName);

    message(UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share" && Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency == "None" && RevenueType != "Variable", "Minimum Commit True Up Frequency must be selected; 'None' is not a valid option. Please select a valid option before saving.", "error");

    message((RevenueType == "Variable" || UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share") && qty != 1, "When Revenue Type is Variable or Unit of Measure is Revenue Share , quantity must be set to 1.", "error");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share", "hide", "attribute", "Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share", "hide", "attribute", "Rev_Share1");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share" || (RevenueType == "Variable" && UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share"), "hide", "attribute", "Monthly_Minimum1");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share" || (RevenueType == "Variable" && UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share"), "hide", "attribute", "Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency");

    message(UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share" && Rev_Share1 <= 0, "Rev Share % is Required and can't be 0", "error");

    message(UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share" && Monthly_Minimum1 <= 0 && RevenueType == "Fixed", "Minimum Commit should be greater than $0 if Revenue Type is Fixed", "error");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure == "Input Records", "disable", "attribute", "Input_Records_V2");

    rule(true, "hide", "attribute", "UnitOfMeasure", "value", "Tokens");

    rule(true, "hide", "attribute", "UnitOfMeasure", "value", "CPM");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure != "Input Records", "hide", "attribute", "Input_Records_V2");

    message(UnitOfMeasure == "Input Records" && Input_Records_V2 == "None", "Please select a valid Input Records value.", "warning");

    message(UnitOfMeasure != "Input Records" && Input_Records_V2 != "None", "If 'Input Records' is not selected on 'Unit of Measure', 'Input Records' must be set to 'None' ", "error");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure != "Input Records" -> Input_Records_V2 == "None");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure == "Input Records" && qty < 50 -> Input_Records_V2 == "25M Input Records");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure == "Input Records" && qty >= 50 && qty < 75 -> Input_Records_V2 == "50M Input Records");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure == "Input Records" && qty >= 75 && qty < 100 -> Input_Records_V2 == "75M Input Records");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure == "Input Records" && qty >= 100 && qty < 250 -> Input_Records_V2 == "100M Input Records");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure == "Input Records" && qty >= 250 && qty < 500 -> Input_Records_V2 == "250M Input Records");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure == "Input Records" && qty >= 500 && qty < 750 -> Input_Records_V2 == "500M Input Records");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure == "Input Records" && qty >= 750 && qty < 1000 -> Input_Records_V2 == "750M Input Records");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure == "Input Records" && qty == 1000 -> Input_Records_V2 == "1B Input Records");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure == "Input Records" && qty > 1000 -> Input_Records_V2 == "One Partner Unlimited");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share" || RevenueType == "Variable" -> Rev_Share1 == 0 && Monthly_Minimum1 == 0 && Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency == "None");

    message(Pricing_Type__c != 'Token' && UnitOfMeasure == "None", "Please select a valid Unit of Measure.", "error");

    //CRM-43551

    rule(UnitOfMeasure == "Tokens", "hide", "attribute", "UnitOfMeasure");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure == "Tokens", "hide", "attribute", "RevenueType");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure == "CPM", "hide", "attribute", "UnitOfMeasure");

    //CPM

    rule(UnitOfMeasure == "CPM", "hide", "attribute", "RevenueType");

    message(Input_Records_V2 == "One Partner Unlimited", "A support case will be automatically created for below conditions: Input Records (One Partner Unlimited)", "Info");

    //CRM-43819

}

@(peelable = true)
type ActivationAPI : LineItem {
    @(relatedAttributes = "Rev_Share1, Monthly_Minimum1, Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency")
    string RevenueType = ["Fixed", "One-Time", "Variable", "None"];

    @(defaultValue = "0.00", peelable = true)
    decimal(2) Monthly_Minimum1;

    @(defaultValue = "0.00", peelable = true)
    decimal(2) Rev_Share1;

    @(defaultValue = "None", peelable = true)
    string Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency = ["Monthly", "Quarterly", "Annual", "None"];

    @(relatedAttributes = "Rev_Share1, Monthly_Minimum1, Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency")
    string UnitOfMeasure = ["Revenue Share", "Each", "Tokens", "Impressions", "None", "CPM"];

    string Cost_Tier = ["Tier 1: Bundled", "None", "Tier 2: Standard", "Tier 3: Advanced", "Tier 4: Embedded", "Tier 5: CDP Whitelabel"];

    string Impressions = ["None", "30M Impressions", "65M Impressions", "150M Impressions", "260M Impressions", "400M Impressions", "575M Impressions", "850M Impressions", "1B Impressions", ">1B Impressions"];

    int qty = this.quantity;

    string parentName = parent(BundleName);

    rule(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share", "hide", "attribute", "Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share", "hide", "attribute", "Rev_Share1");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share" || (RevenueType == "Variable" && UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share"), "hide", "attribute", "Monthly_Minimum1");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share" || (RevenueType == "Variable" && UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share"), "hide", "attribute", "Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency");

    message(UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share" && Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency == "None" && RevenueType != "Variable", "Minimum Commit True Up Frequency must be selected; 'None' is not a valid option. Please select a valid option before saving.", "error");

    // message((RevenueType == "Variable" || UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share") && qty != 1, "When Revenue Type is Variable or Unit of Measure is Revenue Share , quantity must be set to 1.", "error");

    message(qty != 1, "Quantity should be 1 for this Product.", "error");

    message(UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share" && Rev_Share1 <= 0, "Rev Share % is Required and can't be 0", "error");

    message(UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share" && Monthly_Minimum1 <= 0 && RevenueType == "Fixed", "Minimum Commit should be greater than $0 if Revenue Type is Fixed", "error");

    message(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share" && Rev_Share1 != 0, "Rev Share % must be 0 if Revenue Share is not selected in Unit of Measure.", "error");

    message(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share" && Monthly_Minimum1 != 0, "Minimum Commit must be 0 if Revenue Share is not selected in Unit of Measure.", "error");

    rule(true, "hide", "attribute", "UnitOfMeasure", "value", "CPM");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure != "Impressions", "hide", "attribute", "Impressions");

    message(UnitOfMeasure == "Impressions" && Impressions == "None", "Please select a valid Impressions value.", "warning");

    // message(UnitOfMeasure != "Impressions" && Impressions != "None", "If 'Impressions' is not selected on 'Unit of Measure', 'Impressions' must be set to 'None' ", "error");

    message(UnitOfMeasure == "Each" && Cost_Tier == "None", "Please select a valid Cost Tier value.", "warning");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share" || RevenueType == "Variable" -> Rev_Share1 == 0 && Monthly_Minimum1 == 0 && Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency == "None");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure != "Each" -> Cost_Tier == "None");

    message(UnitOfMeasure == "None", "Please select a valid Unit of Measure.", "error");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share" -> RevenueType == "Fixed");

type TransactionResolution : LineItem {
    string RevenueType = ["Fixed", "One-Time", "Variable", "None"];

    int qty = this.quantity;

    string UnitOfMeasure = ["Input Records", "Tokens", "CPM"];

    string Input_Records_V2 = ["None", "500M Input Records", "1B Input Records", "5M Input Records", "10M Input Records", "150M Input Records", "300M Input Records", "20M Input Records", "40M Input Records", "80M Input Records", "2B Input Records", "5B Input Records", "10B Input Records", "10B+ Input Records"];

    rule(true, "hide", "attribute", "UnitOfMeasure", "value", "Tokens");

    rule(true, "hide", "attribute", "UnitOfMeasure", "value", "CPM");

    message(RevenueType == "Variable" && qty != 1, "When Revenue Type is Variable, quantity must be set to 1.", "error");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure == "Input Records", "disable", "attribute", "Input_Records_V2");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure == "Input Records" && qty < 10 -> Input_Records_V2 == "5M Input Records");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure == "Input Records" && qty >= 10 && qty < 20 -> Input_Records_V2 == "10M Input Records");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure == "Input Records" && qty >= 20 && qty < 40 -> Input_Records_V2 == "20M Input Records");

    message(UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share" && Monthly_Minimum1 <= 0 && RevenueType == "Fixed", "Minimum Commit should be greater than $0 if Revenue Type is Fixed", "error");

    message(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share" && Rev_Share1 != 0, "Rev Share % must be 0 if Revenue Share is not selected in Unit of Measure.", "error");

    message(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share" && Monthly_Minimum1 != 0, "Minimum Commit must be 0 if Revenue Share is not selected in Unit of Measure.", "error");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share" || RevenueType == "Variable" -> Rev_Share1 == 0 && Monthly_Minimum1 == 0 && Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency == "None");

    message(UnitOfMeasure == "None", "Please select a valid Unit of Measure.", "error");

    message(true, "$Label.CustomPriceInfo", "Info");

    //CRM-43819

}

    @(defaultValue = "None", peelable = true)
    string Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency = ["None", "Monthly", "Quarterly", "Annual"];

    @(relatedAttributes = "Rev_Share1, Monthly_Minimum1, Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency")
    string UnitOfMeasure = ["Revenue Share", "Each"];

    rule(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share", "hide", "attribute", "Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share", "hide", "attribute", "Rev_Share1");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share" || (RevenueType == "Variable" && UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share"), "hide", "attribute", "Monthly_Minimum1");

    rule(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share" || (RevenueType == "Variable" && UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share"), "hide", "attribute", "Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency");

    message(UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share" && Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency == "None" && RevenueType != "Variable", "Minimum Commit True Up Frequency must be selected; 'None' is not a valid option. Please select a valid option before saving.", "error");

    // message((RevenueType == "Variable" || UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share") && qty != 1, "When Revenue Type is Variable or Unit of Measure is Revenue Share , quantity must be set to 1.", "error");

    message(UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share" && Rev_Share1 <= 0, "Rev Share % is Required and can't be 0", "error");

    message(UnitOfMeasure == "Revenue Share" && Monthly_Minimum1 <= 0 && RevenueType == "Fixed", "Minimum Commit should be greater than $0 if Revenue Type is Fixed", "error");

    message(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share" && Rev_Share1 != 0, "Rev Share % must be 0 if Revenue Share is not selected in Unit of Measure.", "error");

    message(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share" && Monthly_Minimum1 != 0, "Minimum Commit must be 0 if Revenue Share is not selected in Unit of Measure.", "error");

    preference(UnitOfMeasure != "Revenue Share" || RevenueType == "Variable" -> Rev_Share1 == 0 && Monthly_Minimum1 == 0 && Minimum_Commit_True_Up_Frequency == "None");

    message(true, "$Label.CustomPriceInfo", "Info");

    //CRM-43819

}
